- **jj-security-review**: Security review of the pending changes on the current branch in a jj repo. Use when the user asks for a security review (/security-review, /jj-security-review) and the session is in a jj workspace or colocated jj checkout — the built-in /security-review shells out to git, which is blocked in Claude Code jj workspaces (no .git, GIT_CEILING_DIRECTORIES).
